- **spec-reviewer**: Checks behaviour, schemas, and delivery claims against the numbered ContextOS specification. Use before closing a requirement or claiming a phase gate. ([zentelligence/context-os-mcp](https://github.com/zentelligence/context-os-mcp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Use to review an hns spec from ONE dimension (architecture, domain, implementation, security, test-strategy, or usecase) in a fresh, read-only context. Spawn one per dimension in parallel. Returns SHIP / REVISE / BLOCK with file:line evidence. ([1989v/ai](https://github.com/1989v/ai/blob/HEAD/plugins/hns/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Stage 1 of the SDD two-stage review gate. Runs after every implementer run and BEFORE code-reviewer. Verifies the implementation matches the spec exactly — nothing more, nothing less — by reading actual code, not trusting the implementer's report. Does NOT evaluate code quality. Read-only. Adapted from obra/superpowers SDD. ([Abstract-Data/campaignfinance-2023](https://github.com/Abstract-Data/campaignfinance-2023/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Requirements analyst that reviews docs/project_spec.md for completeness, clarity, and scope discipline. Use proactively before any building starts, and after major additions to the spec. ([AleCaballeroM/AIBuilder](https://github.com/AleCaballeroM/AIBuilder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only review specialist. Use to review a change for correctness, security, performance, convention adherence, and (for UI) design-spec conformance. Returns prioritized, evidence-backed findings with file:line references. Never edits code — it judges, it does not fix. ([AnandSGit/Healer](https://github.com/AnandSGit/Healer/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only auditor for spec.md and plan.md. Runs EARS-conformance + plan-tag audits, returns a compact verdict + findings list. Non-interactive — the orchestrator owns the human review loop. Keeps full file contents out of the orchestrator context. ([AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor](https://github.com/AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews specifications for completeness and testability. Independent validation. ([BryceEWatson/bryce-labs-toolkit](https://github.com/BryceEWatson/bryce-labs-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-workflow/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Stage-one review. Read-only. Checks an implementer's diff strictly against the task's acceptance criteria. Runs before quality-reviewer. ([Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates](https://github.com/Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness-base/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Spec compliance reviewer. Verifies implementation matches plan requirements exactly -- nothing missing, nothing extra. ([Claudfather/clauDNA](https://github.com/Claudfather/clauDNA/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Judges whether one written specification may enter development — free of self-contradiction, current with the code, complete from itself alone, and testable — against the closed admission register. Judges only; holds no editing tools. Runs as the refine judge on its own, or as a child of spec-reviewer-lead. Runs before the pipeline, never inside it. ([Devscribed/Devscribed.Admin](https://github.com/Devscribed/Devscribed.Admin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews an OpenSpec change (proposal.md, design.md, specs/*/spec.md, tasks.md) for internal consistency, testable requirements, and alignment with this project's Spec Driven Development standards. Use when asked to review a spec, review a change proposal, or before implementing/archiving an OpenSpec change. Read-only — never edits files. ([EvgeniyKrishtopa/Github-search-app](https://github.com/EvgeniyKrishtopa/Github-search-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a ticket-worker's actual worktree against its originating ticket or specification without changing implementation files. ([Fatih0234/mattpocock-skills-pi](https://github.com/Fatih0234/mattpocock-skills-pi/blob/HEAD/pi/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Staff-engineer review of the latest commit's diff against the Prism Renderer Migration spec. MUST be invoked at the end of every Ralph iteration (step 9 of /ralph-step). Returns a structured report; never modifies files. ([Flickinny11/Design-trials](https://github.com/Flickinny11/Design-trials/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews implementation against task specification. Checks for missing requirements, extras, and misunderstandings. ([GigaFyde/project-orchestrator](https://github.com/GigaFyde/project-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer-lead**: Judges whether one written specification may enter development, at a scale one context cannot hold — splits the admission register's families across spec-reviewer children, holds contradiction and scope itself, checks what comes back and signs one verdict. Judges only; holds no editing tools. Runs before the pipeline, never inside it. ([Devscribed/Devscribed.Admin](https://github.com/Devscribed/Devscribed.Admin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer-lead.md))
- **spec-reviewer-prompt**: Use this template when dispatching a spec compliance reviewer subagent. ([Bang-isme/CodexAI---Skills](https://github.com/Bang-isme/CodexAI---Skills/blob/HEAD/skills/codex-subagent-execution/agents/spec-reviewer-prompt.md) · ★8)
- **spec-reviser**: Re-runs a Socratic interview against an existing spec.md (not a blank template). Use during /speccraft:spec:revise. ([DCSTOLF/speccraft](https://github.com/DCSTOLF/speccraft/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviser.md) · ★2)
- **spec-reviser**: Spec revision subagent. Takes an existing spec path + a change request (from spec-reviewer findings or user feedback in /solve mode) and produces a revised spec in clean context. Used by uberdev:orchestrator when REVISIONS_REQUIRED. ([UberDevCorp/UberDev](https://github.com/UberDevCorp/UberDev/blob/HEAD/plugins/uberdev/agents/spec-reviser.md) · ★2)
- **spec-risk**: Субагент для анализа рисков в спецификациях. НЕ вызывай напрямую — используется через /spec-review команду. Анализирует: технические риски, бизнес-риски, зависимости от внешних факторов, что может пойти не так, mitigation strategies. ([dapi/claude-code-marketplace](https://github.com/dapi/claude-code-marketplace/blob/HEAD/spec-reviewer/agents/spec-risk.md) · ★16)
